2026-10-19T04:16:37.0242368Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Serve the Prefill bundle image with the prologue gate comparing the old fused kernel within rounding bounds

Run 39194911829 (f98c500) built the Prefill bundle image (39194911829.sqsh,
b8531630) and stopped in the pre-serving numeric gate on all four ranks with
AssertionError ('prefill_128_torch.int32_exact_k', 'not exact') from
check_prefill_attention (scripts/check-firmus-decode-kernels.py). Every
earlier check passed, including the Decode SiLU/quant, RoPE, fused KV, Top-K
and the Prefill-only add-RMSNorm + group quant screens.

The gate log shows which side moved: for 128 rows the new Prefill prologue
was bitwise equal to the unfused reference (fused_inplace_qknorm +
apply_rope_inplace + torch FP8 cast) for Q, K and V
(prefill_128_torch.int32_unfused_{q,k,v}: bitwise_equal true) and bitwise
equal to the existing fused kernel for Q, while the existing fused kernel's
BF16 K writeback cast to FP8 differed from both (relative L2 1.2e-4, max
scaled error 5.7e-3, i.e. a single FP8 step on very few elements). The
existing kernel already deviates from the unfused reference in its own
screens (rope_k_normTrue_* lines). The SASS of the old kernel, compiled from
the new image on the Firmus login node, runs the same FFMA sum of squares,
butterfly reduction, rsqrt and RoPE FFMA forms for Q and K, so this is a
rounding-level difference in the old path, not an indexing or race defect in
the new kernel: a race or wrong row in the new kernel could not stay bitwise
equal to the unfused reference.

The byte-for-byte comparison with the old fused kernel was stricter than the
precision rule, which accepts floating-point ordering/FMA differences and
forbids only lower precision or skipped work. It now records that distance
with check_numerics under the same bounds as the unfused-reference check.
Unchanged and still exact: V, the paged K/V cache rows written by the fused
store versus store_cache, and the input-unmodified assertion; the
unfused-reference bounds and the FA3 paged-vs-contiguous comparison are also
unchanged. The remaining rows (129 .. 4096), graph replays and the MegaMoE
ping-pong numerics that the failure cut off run in this job.

Only the gate script and the image pin change, so this run reuses the image
built by 39194911829 (configs/firmus-framework.json images.sglang) instead of
rebuilding; the kernel chain, cap 128, concurrency 192 and runtime switches
are those of f98c500.

Validation: ruff and pytest pass.
